# Notebook colour contrast -- measured

Generated by `app/src/pages/journal-2-0/a11y/notebookContrast.test.js` at `deff3c9d4` (wave 8, lane 8A, A5).
Do not edit by hand; regenerate:

```sh
cd app && NOTEBOOK_CONTRAST_DOC=1 npx vitest run src/pages/journal-2-0/a11y/notebookContrast.test.js
```

**Method.** Every rule in the 70 derived Notebook stylesheets (`a11y/cssAudit.js deriveNotebookCss`),
comments stripped. TEXT: the rule's `color` against its own background, else against `--bg`, `--bg-surface`
and `--bg-elevated` (worst of the three); translucent colours composited onto the surface first. Bars:
4.5:1 normal text, 3:1 large text (>= 24px, or >= 18.66px at weight 700+, read from the same rule),
3:1 non-text UI (focus indicators, input borders, the graph selection ring). Tokens are read from
`app/src/styles/tokens.css` `:root` (dark), `[data-theme="oled"]` and `[data-theme="light"]`, `var()` chains resolved.
The ONE formula is `app/src/styles/__tests__/contrastMath.js`.

**Counts.** 680 measured pairs x 3 themes; 11 keyword colours (inherit, currentColor, transparent) counted, not measured.

## Expected failures (need a tokens.css ruling)

- **D-A4-1** (42 pair-theme rows) -- Danger text: --loss (and --color-danger, which is --loss) reads 4.01:1 on --bg-elevated in the dark theme, 3.63-3.73:1 on its own --loss-bg tint, and 4.21-4.30:1 on that tint in oled -- under 4.5:1 for the 10-13px text it is used at. No existing token passes in all three themes (--ut-red-bright is 4.58:1 dark but 3.3:1 light). Proposed: lift the dark/oled --loss lightness in tokens.css (the light theme already passes).
- **D-A4-2** (6 pair-theme rows) -- Success text: --gain on its own --gain-bg tint reads 4.42:1 in the light theme. Proposed: darken the light --gain a step in tokens.css.
- **D-A4-3** (90 pair-theme rows) -- Input boundaries: every Notebook input, select and textarea draws its edge in --border, 1.18:1 (dark) against the page -- WCAG 1.4.11 asks 3:1 for the visual information that identifies a control. --border-accent does not reach it either. Proposed: an input-edge value in tokens.css, or a ruling that these fields are identified by their label and placeholder instead.

## Measured over a picture, not the page

These are composited over black AND white (the extremes of the image underneath):

- `components/notebook/HeroImagePicker.module.css .iconBtn` -- Replace / Remove buttons drawn over the note's hero IMAGE
- `components/notebook/HeroImagePicker.module.css .dropHint` -- the "drop to replace" hint drawn over the hero IMAGE while a file is dragged
- `components/notebook/WidgetEmbedView.module.css .asOfChip` -- the as-of date drawn over a chart SNAPSHOT (its time-axis corner)
